\displaystyle|m\rangle\rightarrow\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),m(R_{z})}}}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{+}|m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}\rangle\rightarrow|m\rangle

\displaystyle III:

\displaystyle|m\rangle\rightarrow\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}),m(R_{z}^{\prime})}}}S_{R_{z}}^{+}|m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}\rangle\rightarrow S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{-}S_{R_{z}}^{+}|m\rangle

\displaystyle IV:

\displaystyle|m\rangle\rightarrow\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),m(R_{z})}}}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{+}|m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}\rangle\rightarrow S_{R_{z}}^{-}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{+}|m\rangle

\displaystyle\text{with }R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1

(S14.279)

We now evaluate
H_{SW}
with contributions from each virtual procedure.

XIV.2.1
Contribution from I

We take

\displaystyle|L,j\rangle=|m\rangle,\quad|L,i\rangle=|m\rangle

\displaystyle|H,n\rangle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}),m(R_{z}^{\prime})}}}S_{R_{z}}^{+}|m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}\rangle

(S14.280)

The corresponding
V_{in}
(from Eq.
S14.268
and Eq.
S14.270
) is

\displaystyle V_{in}=(V_{ni}^{*})=\begin{cases}-\alpha_{S,m(R_{z})}\alpha_{S,m(R_{z}^{\prime})}(-1)^{N_{xy}}f^{*}_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}(1-\delta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),-S})(S-m(R_{z}))\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}),m(R_{z}^{\prime})}}}{S^{2}-m(R_{z})^{2}}&R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}+1\\
\alpha_{S,m(R_{z})}\alpha_{S,m(R_{z}^{\prime})}(-1)^{N_{xy}}f^{*}_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}(1-\delta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),-S})(S-m(R_{z}))\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}),m(R_{z}^{\prime})}}}{S^{2}-m(R_{z})^{2}}&R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}-1\end{cases}

(S14.281)

The contribution to the
H_{SW}
then reads

\displaystyle H_{SW}^{I}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{1}{2}\bigg|\alpha_{S,m(R_{z})}\alpha_{S,m(R_{z}^{\prime})}(-1)^{N_{xy}}f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}(1-\delta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),-S})(S-m(R_{z}))\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}),m(R_{z}^{\prime})}}}{S^{2}-m(R_{z})^{2}}\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{1}{-E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}+\frac{1}{-E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}\bigg)|m\rangle\langle m|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}}{E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}\prod_{i=m(R_{z})+1}^{S-1}\frac{S^{2}-i^{2}}{S(S+1)-i(i+1)}\prod_{j=m(R_{z}^{\prime})}^{S-1}\frac{S^{2}-j^{2}}{S(S+1)-j(j+1)}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S(S+1)-m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})+1)}(1-\delta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),-S})(S-m(R_{z}))^{2}|m\rangle\langle m|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}}{E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}\frac{(S+m(R_{z}^{\prime}))(S-m(R_{z}))}{4S^{2}}|m\rangle\langle m|

(S14.282)

XIV.2.2
Contribution from II

We take

\displaystyle|L,j\rangle=|m\rangle,\quad|L,i\rangle=|m\rangle

\displaystyle|H,n\rangle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),m(R_{z})}}}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{+}|m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}\rangle

(S14.283)

The corresponding
V_{in}
(from Eq.
S14.268
and Eq.
S14.270
) is

\displaystyle V_{in}=(V_{ni}^{*})=\begin{cases}\alpha_{S,m(R_{z})}\alpha_{S,m(R_{z}^{\prime})}(-1)^{N_{xy}}f^{*}_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}(1-\delta_{m(R_{z}),-S})(S-m(R_{z}^{\prime}))\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),m(R_{z})}}}{S^{2}-m(R_{z}^{\prime})^{2}}&R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}+1\\
-\alpha_{S,m(R_{z})}\alpha_{S,m(R_{z}^{\prime})}(-1)^{N_{xy}}f^{*}_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}(1-\delta_{m(R_{z}),-S})(S-m(R_{z}^{\prime}))\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),m(R_{z})}}}{S^{2}-m(R_{z}^{\prime})^{2}}&R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}-1\end{cases}

(S14.284)

The contribution to the
H_{SW}
then reads

\displaystyle H_{SW}^{II}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{1}{2}\bigg|\alpha_{S,m(R_{z})}\alpha_{S,m(R_{z}^{\prime})}(-1)^{N_{xy}}f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}(1-\delta_{m(R_{z}),-S})(S-m(R_{z}^{\prime}))\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),m(R_{z})}}}{S^{2}-m(R_{z}^{\prime})^{2}}\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{1}{-E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}+\frac{1}{-E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}\bigg)|m\rangle\langle m|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}}{E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}\prod_{i=m(R_{z}^{\prime})+1}^{S-1}\frac{S^{2}-i^{2}}{S(S+1)-i(i+1)}\prod_{j=m(R_{z})}^{S-1}\frac{S^{2}-j^{2}}{S(S+1)-j(j+1)}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{S(S+1)-m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})+1)}(1-\delta_{m(R_{z}),-S})(S-m(R_{z}^{\prime}))^{2}|m\rangle\langle m|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}}{E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}\frac{(S+m(R_{z}))(S-m(R_{z}^{\prime}))}{4S^{2}}|m\rangle\langle m|

(S14.285)

XIV.2.3
Contribution from III

We take

\displaystyle|L,j\rangle=|m\rangle,\quad|L,i\rangle=|\tilde{m}\rangle

\displaystyle|H,n\rangle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}),m(R_{z}^{\prime})}}}S_{R_{z}}^{+}|m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}\rangle,\quad R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1

(S14.286)

where
|\tilde{m}\rangle
denotes the ground state obtained by acting
S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{-}S_{R_{z}}^{+}
on the
|m\rangle

\displaystyle|\tilde{m}\rangle=\frac{S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{-}S_{R_{z}}^{+}}{\sqrt{[S(S+1)-m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})-1)][S(S+1)-m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})+1)]}}|m\rangle

(S14.287)

where the additional coefficient in the definition of
|\tilde{m}\rangle
has been introduced to ensure
\langle\tilde{m}|\tilde{m}\rangle=1
(see also Eq.
S14.213
). We also comment that, the contribution from
S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{+}S_{R_{z}}^{-}|m\rangle
can be obtained by switching the position indices
R_{z}^{\prime}
and
R_{z}
in Eq.
S14.287
.
In practice, use the notation introduced in Eq.
S14.217
, we find

\displaystyle\tilde{m}(R)=\begin{cases}m(R)&R\neq R_{z},R\neq R_{z}^{\prime}\\
m(R)+1&R=R_{z}\\
m(R)-1&R=R_{z}^{\prime}\end{cases}

(S14.288)

The corresponding
V_{in},V_{jn}^{*}
(from Eq.
S14.268
) are then

\displaystyle V_{in}=(V_{ni}^{*})=\begin{cases}-\alpha_{S,m(R_{z})}\alpha_{S,m(R_{z}^{\prime})}(-1)^{N_{xy}}f^{*}_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}(1-\delta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),-S})(S-m(R_{z}))\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}),m(R_{z}^{\prime})}}}{S^{2}-m(R_{z})^{2}}&R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}+1\\
\alpha_{S,m(R_{z})}\alpha_{S,m(R_{z}^{\prime})}(-1)^{N_{xy}}f^{*}_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}(1-\delta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),-S})(S-m(R_{z}))\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}),m(R_{z}^{\prime})}}}{S^{2}-m(R_{z})^{2}}&R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}-1\end{cases}

\displaystyle V_{nj}=\begin{cases}\alpha_{S,\tilde{m}(R_{z})}\alpha_{S,\tilde{m}(R_{z}^{\prime})}(-1)^{N_{xy}}f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}(1-\delta_{\tilde{m}(R_{z}),-S})(S-\tilde{m}(R_{z}^{\prime}))\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{\tilde{m}(R_{z}^{\prime}),\tilde{m}(R_{z})}}}{S^{2}-\tilde{m}(R_{z}^{\prime})^{2}}&R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}+1\\
-\alpha_{S,\tilde{m}(R_{z})}\alpha_{S,\tilde{m}(R_{z}^{\prime})}(-1)^{N_{xy}}f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}(1-\delta_{\tilde{m}(R_{z}),-S})(S-\tilde{m}(R_{z}^{\prime}))\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{\tilde{m}(R_{z}^{\prime}),\tilde{m}(R_{z})}}}{S^{2}-\tilde{m}(R_{z}^{\prime})^{2}}&R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}-1\end{cases}

(S14.289)

The contribution to the
H_{SW}
then reads

\displaystyle H_{SW}^{III}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{-E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}+\frac{1}{-E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}\bigg)|m\rangle\langle\tilde{m}|

\displaystyle\alpha_{S,m(R_{z})}\alpha_{S,m(R_{z}^{\prime})}\alpha_{S,\tilde{m}(R_{z})}\alpha_{S,\tilde{m}(R_{z}^{\prime})}(-1)|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}(1-\delta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),-S})(1-\delta_{\tilde{m}(R_{z}),-S})(S-m(R_{z}))(S-\tilde{m}(R_{z}^{\prime}))

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}),m(R_{z}^{\prime})}\eta_{\tilde{m}(R_{z}^{\prime}),\tilde{m}(R_{z})}}}{(S^{2}-m(R_{z})^{2})(S^{2}-\tilde{m}(R_{z}^{\prime})^{2})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}}{E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}|m\rangle\langle\tilde{m}|(S-m({R_{z}}))(S-m({R_{z}^{\prime}})-1)

\displaystyle\prod_{i=m(R_{z})+1}^{S-1}\frac{S^{2}-i^{2}}{S(S+1)-i(i+1)}\prod_{j=m(R_{z}^{\prime})}^{S-1}\frac{S^{2}-i^{2}}{S(S+1)-i(i+1)}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})+1)}\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})-1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}}{E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}\frac{\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})+1)}\sqrt{S(S+1)+m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})-1)}}{4S^{2}}|m\rangle\langle\tilde{m}|

(S14.290)

XIV.2.4
Contribution from IV

We take

\displaystyle|L,j\rangle=|m\rangle,\quad|L,i\rangle=|\tilde{\tilde{m}}\rangle

\displaystyle|H,n\rangle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),m(R_{z})}}}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{+}|m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}\rangle,\quad R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1

(S14.291)

where
|\tilde{\tilde{m}}\rangle
denotes the ground state obtained by acting
S_{R_{z}}^{-}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{+}
on the
|m\rangle

\displaystyle|\tilde{\tilde{m}}\rangle=\frac{S_{R_{z}}^{-}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{+}}{\sqrt{[S(S+1)-m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})-1)][S(S+1)-m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})+1)]}}|m\rangle

(S14.292)

where the additional coefficient in the definition of
|\tilde{\tilde{m}}\rangle
has been introduced to ensure
\langle\tilde{\tilde{m}}|\tilde{\tilde{m}}\rangle=1
(see also Eq.
S14.213
). In practice, using the notation introduced in Eq.
S14.217
, we find

\displaystyle\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R)=\begin{cases}m(R)&R\neq R_{z},R\neq R_{z}^{\prime}\\
m(R)-1&R=R_{z}\\
m(R)+1&R=R_{z}^{\prime}\end{cases}

(S14.293)

The corresponding
V_{in},V_{jn}^{*}
(from Eq.
S14.268
) are then

\displaystyle V_{in}=(V_{ni}^{*})=\begin{cases}-\alpha_{S,m(R_{z}^{\prime})}\alpha_{S,m(R_{z})}(-1)^{N_{xy}}f^{*}_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}(1-\delta_{m(R_{z}),-S})(S-m(R_{z}^{\prime}))\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),m(R_{z})}}}{S^{2}-m(R_{z}^{\prime})^{2}}&R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}-1\\
\alpha_{S,m(R_{z}^{\prime})}\alpha_{S,m(R_{z})}(-1)^{N_{xy}}f^{*}_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}(1-\delta_{m(R_{z}),-S})(S-m(R_{z}^{\prime}))\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),m(R_{z})}}}{S^{2}-m(R_{z}^{\prime})^{2}}&R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}+1\end{cases}

\displaystyle V_{nj}=\begin{cases}\alpha_{S,\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}^{\prime})}\alpha_{S,\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z})}(-1)^{N_{xy}}f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}(1-\delta_{\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}^{\prime}),-S})(S-\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}))\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}),\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}^{\prime})}}}{S^{2}-\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z})^{2}}&R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}-1\\
-\alpha_{S,\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}^{\prime})}\alpha_{S,\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z})}(-1)^{N_{xy}}f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}(1-\delta_{\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}^{\prime}),-S})(S-\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}))\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}),\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}^{\prime})}}}{S^{2}-\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z})^{2}}&R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}+1\end{cases}

(S14.294)

The contribution to the
H_{SW}
then reads

\displaystyle H_{SW}^{IV}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{-E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}+\frac{1}{-E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}\bigg)|m\rangle\langle\tilde{\tilde{m}}|

\displaystyle\alpha_{S,m(R_{z}^{\prime})}\alpha_{S,m(R_{z})}\alpha_{S,\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}^{\prime})}\alpha_{S,\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z})}(-1)|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}(1-\delta_{m(R_{z}),-S})(1-\delta_{\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}^{\prime}),-S})(S-m(R_{z}^{\prime}))(S-\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}))

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\eta_{m(R_{z}^{\prime}),m(R_{z})}\eta_{\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}),\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z}^{\prime})}}}{(S^{2}-m(R_{z}^{\prime})^{2})(S^{2}-\tilde{\tilde{m}}(R_{z})^{2})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}}{E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}|m\rangle\langle\tilde{\tilde{m}}|(S-m({R_{z}^{\prime}}))(S-m({R_{z}})-1)

\displaystyle\prod_{i=m(R_{z}^{\prime})+1}^{S-1}\frac{S^{2}-i^{2}}{S(S+1)-i(i+1)}\prod_{j=m(R_{z})}^{S-1}\frac{S^{2}-i^{2}}{S(S+1)-i(i+1)}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})+1)}\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})-1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}}{E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}\frac{\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})+1)}\sqrt{S(S+1)+m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})-1)}}{4S^{2}}|m\rangle\langle\tilde{\tilde{m}}|

(S14.295)

XIV.2.5
Effective spin-spin interaction terms

We combine Eq.
S14.282
, Eq.
S14.285
, Eq.
S14.290
, Eq.
S14.295
, and find the additional term generated by Schrieffer-Wolff transformation is

\displaystyle H_{SW}=

\displaystyle H_{SW}^{I}+H_{SW}^{II}+H_{SW}^{III}+H_{SW}^{IV}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}}{E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}\frac{-S^{2}+m(R_{z})m(R_{z}^{\prime})}{2S^{2}}|m\rangle\langle m|

\displaystyle\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}}{E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}\bigg[\frac{\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})+1)}\sqrt{S(S+1)+m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})-1)}}{4S^{2}}|m\rangle\langle\tilde{m}|

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})+1)}\sqrt{S(S+1)+m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})-1)}}{4S^{2}}|m\rangle\langle\tilde{\tilde{m}}|\bigg]

(S14.296)

where
|\tilde{m}\rangle,|\tilde{\tilde{m}}\rangle
states are defined via Eq.
S14.288
and Eq.
S14.293
respectively.

In a more compact formula, we find

\displaystyle H_{SW}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}}{E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}\frac{-S^{2}+m(R_{z})m(R_{z}^{\prime})}{2S^{2}}|m\rangle\langle m|

\displaystyle+\sum_{m,\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=R_{z}\pm 1}\frac{|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}}{E_{\mathbf{k}^{xy}}^{exct}}\bigg[\frac{\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})+1)}\sqrt{S(S+1)+m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})-1)}}{4S^{2}}\bigg[|m\rangle\langle\tilde{m}|+|\tilde{m}\rangle\langle{m}|\bigg]

(S14.297)

We can rewrite Eq.
S14.297
in a more compact form via the following spin operators (Eq.
S14.210
)

\displaystyle S^{x,y,z}_{R_{z}}=\sum_{\mathbf{k}^{xy},\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}}\gamma_{\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},\sigma}^{\dagger}\frac{\sigma^{x,y,z}_{\sigma\sigma^{\prime}}}{2}\gamma_{\mathbf{k}^{xy},R_{z},\sigma^{\prime}}

\displaystyle S^{+}_{R_{z}}=S_{R_{z}}^{x}+iS_{R_{z}}^{y}

\displaystyle S^{-}_{R_{z}}=S_{R_{z}}^{x}-iS_{R_{z}}^{y}

(S14.298)

We find

\displaystyle S_{R_{z}}^{z}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{z}|m\rangle=m(R_{z})m(R_{z}^{\prime})|m\rangle

(S14.299)

Thus, acting on the ground state manifolds, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{m}m(R_{z})m(R_{z}^{\prime})|m\rangle\langle m|=S_{R_{z}}^{z}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{z}

(S14.300)

Also, we find

\displaystyle S_{R_{z}}^{+}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{-}|m\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg[\bigotimes_{R_{z}^{\prime\prime}\neq R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime\prime}\neq R_{z}^{\prime}}|R_{z}^{\prime\prime},m(R_{z}^{\prime\prime})\rangle\bigg]S_{R_{z}}^{+}|R_{z},m(R_{z})\rangle S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{-}|R_{z}^{\prime},m(R_{z}^{\prime})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigotimes_{R_{z}^{\prime\prime}\neq R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime\prime}\neq R_{z}^{\prime}}|R_{z}^{\prime\prime},m(R_{z}^{\prime\prime})\rangle\bigg]\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})+1)}|m(R_{z})+1\rangle\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})-1)}|m(R_{z}^{\prime})-1\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})+1)}\sqrt{S(S+1)+m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})-1)}|\tilde{m}\rangle

(S14.301)

Therefore, we have the following identity

\displaystyle\sum_{m}|\tilde{m}\rangle\langle m|=\frac{1}{\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})+1)}\sqrt{S(S+1)+m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})-1)}}S_{R_{z}}^{+}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{-}

(S14.302)

In addition,

\displaystyle S_{R_{z}}^{-}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{+}|\tilde{m}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg[\bigotimes_{R_{z}^{\prime\prime}\neq R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime\prime}\neq R_{z}^{\prime}}|R_{z}^{\prime\prime},m(R_{z}^{\prime\prime})\rangle\bigg]S_{R_{z}}^{-}|R_{z},m(R_{z})+1\rangle S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{+}|R_{z}^{\prime},m(R_{z}^{\prime})-1\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigotimes_{R_{z}^{\prime\prime}\neq R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime\prime}\neq R_{z}^{\prime}}|R_{z}^{\prime\prime},m(R_{z}^{\prime\prime})\rangle\bigg]\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})+1)}|m(R_{z})\rangle\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})-1)}|m(R_{z}^{\prime})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})+1)}\sqrt{S(S+1)+m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})-1)}|{m}\rangle

(S14.303)

Therefore, we have the following identity

\displaystyle\sum_{m}|{m}\rangle\langle\tilde{m}|=\frac{1}{\sqrt{S(S+1)-m(R_{z})(m(R_{z})+1)}\sqrt{S(S+1)+m(R_{z}^{\prime})(m(R_{z}^{\prime})-1)}}S_{R_{z}}^{-}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{+}

(S14.304)

Combining Eq.
S14.297
, Eq.
S14.300
, Eq.
S14.302
and Eq.
S14.304
,
we could rewrite the
H_{SW}
with the spin operators as

\displaystyle H_{SW}=

\displaystyle\sum_{R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=\pm 1}J\bigg[-S^{2}+S_{R_{z}}^{z}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{z}+\frac{1}{2}S_{R_{z}}^{+}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{-}+\frac{1}{2}S_{R_{z}}^{-}S_{R_{z}^{\prime}}^{+}\bigg]=\sum_{R_{z},R_{z}^{\prime}=\pm 1}J\bigg[-S^{2}+\bm{S}_{R_{z}}\cdot\bm{S}_{R_{z}^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle J=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{k}}\frac{|f_{z,\mathbf{k}^{xy}}|^{2}}{2E_{\mathbf{k}}^{exct}S^{2}}>0

(S14.305)

Therefore, we could observe that the
z
-direction hopping generates inter-layer Heisenberg-types of antiferromagnetic interactions (
J>0
). The antiferromagnetic interactions then stabilize the following type-A antiferromagnetic state

\displaystyle\bigotimes_{R_{z}}|R_{z},(-1)^{R_{z}}S\rangle

(S14.306)

We also note that, by combining all terms,
H^{I}_{SW},H^{II}_{SW},H^{III}_{SW},H^{IV}_{SW}
, we recover the SU(2) symmetry of the system.

Finally, we comment that, we could also introduce an in-plane dispersion to the flat bands. However, as we demonstrated in Ref.
[
108
]
, the in-plane ferromagnetic coupling generated by quantum geometry is proportional to
U
, and the antiferromagnetic coupling generated by the dispersion is proportional to
D^{2}/U
with
D
the bandwidth of the system. Therefore, the in-plane dispersion will not destroy the in-plane ferromagnetism as long as the dispersion is weak.
